## Edge Cases & Boundaries

### POST `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/orders/customer/create` — Order Create — Missing Idempotency-Key (edge)

**Description:** Negative: order creation without the Idempotency-Key header must be rejected with 400. Idempotency keys are mandatory so network retries can never create duplicate orders.

**Result:** `PASS` | **Status:** `400` | **Time:** 13ms

**Request headers:**
```json
{
  "Accept": "application/json",
  "Content-Type": "application/json"
}
```

**Request body:**
```json
{
  "restaurantId": 333,
  "deliveryAddressId": 716,
  "specialInstructions": "Ring the bell",
  "contactlessDelivery": false,
  "paymentMethod": "CASH_ON_DELIVERY",
  "tipAmount": 20.0
}
```

**Response:**
```json
{
  "success": false,
  "message": "Missing required header: Idempotency-Key",
  "data": null,
  "timestamp": "2026-08-25T11:29:46.595808",
  "traceId": "fa3d48c817ac33a35418b18ce7cf8026",
  "spanId": "5848699070114db7",
  "requestId": "7fbefb2d"
}
```

---

### POST `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/orders/customer/create` — Order Create — Blank Idempotency-Key (edge)

**Description:** Negative: an empty Idempotency-Key header is equivalent to missing and must be rejected with 400.

**Result:** `PASS` | **Status:** `400` | **Time:** 12ms

**Request headers:**
```json
{
  "Accept": "application/json",
  "Content-Type": "application/json",
  "Idempotency-Key": ""
}

**Request body:**

  "deliveryAddressId": 716,
  "specialInstructions": "Ring the bell",
  "contactlessDelivery": false,
  "paymentMethod": "CASH_ON_DELIVERY",
  "tipAmount": 20.0
}

**Response:**

  "traceId": "0b7f92e8d1abb690c748729bf5266c82",
  "spanId": "7530793a466caf76",
  "requestId": "111800f2"
}
```
